## Summary Statistics

- **Benchmarks (Yes)**: 235 papers
- **Benchmarks (No)**: 341 papers
- **Evaluations (Yes)**: 365 papers
- **Evaluations (No)**: 211 papers
- **Datasets (Yes)**: 295 papers
- **Datasets (No)**: 281 papers

### Multi-Category Breakdown

- **All Three (Benchmark + Eval + Dataset)**: 122 papers
- **Benchmark + Eval only**: 79 papers
- **Benchmark + Dataset only**: 21 papers
- **Eval + Dataset only**: 90 papers
- **None of the three**: 115 papers

### Metrics Extraction

- **Papers with extracted metrics**: 68 papers
- **Papers without metrics**: 508 papers
